Method: 作者首先在SOFA框架中，以不同刚度的重力加载硅胶梁为对象，系统标定超弹性本构模型；随后利用标定后的有限元仿真生成训练数据。基于这些数据，训练一个以软硬程度为条件输入的等变图神经网络，使其学习节点形变和接触/反作用力，并支持多种组织刚度及未见几何的预测。

Result: 实验表明，该网络可在约0.010秒内完成推理，并取得亚毫米级的平均形变预测精度。模型能够在不同材料刚度与未见几何之间泛化；但力预测的质量与稳定性明显受本构模型标定质量及不同刚度条件下标定一致性的影响。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是将材料本构标定作为学习型软组织代理模型可靠性的关键前提，并通过刚度条件化等变图网络实现快速、跨材料和跨几何的形变预测。其结果说明，高质量形变预测可以实时实现，但若要获得可靠力反馈，必须优先保证上游超弹性模型及参数标定的一致性。

Abstract: Accurate soft tissue simulation is essential for surgical training, pre-operative planning, and haptic feedback systems. While learning-based surrogate models trained on data using the finite element method (FEM) offer a promising path to real-time inference, their reliability depends on well-calibrated constitutive models. Existing approaches neither provide systematic guidance on model selection across stiffness levels, nor generalize across different tissue stiffnesses or geometries. We perform a comprehensive calibration of hyperelastic constitutive models in the SOFA Framework using gravity-loaded silicone beams with different stiffnesses. Using calibrated simulations as training data, we use a softness conditioned equivariant graph neural network, enabling deformation and force prediction across multiple tissue types and unseen geometries. Our model achieves sub-millimeter mean deformation accuracy at 0.010s inference time, while showing that force prediction quality is directly tied to upstream calibration consistency.

</details>


### [70] [Deep Learning Models Also Recall Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20970)
*Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“特征回忆”概念，主张深度学习模型中的线性投影不仅可被理解为特征组合，也可被理解为按输入激活强度检索参数中存储的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有机制可解释性研究主要考察模型如何从权重中回忆事实，但这一视角难以覆盖更一般的信息处理过程。作者希望将“事实回忆”推广为跨架构的通用计算操作，并补充以特征组合为主的既有解释框架。

Method: 论文从线性投影的数学结构出发：将投影矩阵的成分视为存储的信息，将输入激活视为其检索或缩放系数；据此形式化定义特征回忆，讨论其在不同模型架构中的适用性，并提出通过机制层面的表征、权重与激活分析来识别此类过程的思路。

Result: 作者论证线性投影可具有“检索已存储特征”的解释，而非仅是将输入特征混合生成新表示。该观点可推广至多种深度学习架构，并为区分特征回忆与特征组合提供概念基础及潜在的经验检验方向。

Conclusion: “特征回忆”为理解深度学习内部信息流提供了新的概念工具：模型计算可部分表现为由激活条件化的参数信息检索。该框架有望扩展机制可解释性的研究对象，并推动对具体回忆机制的可验证分析。

Abstract: Recent work in mechanistic interpretability has studied how large language models recall facts stored in their weights. This paper argues that factual recall points to something broader: a general kind of operation in deep learning models, which I call feature recall. The core observation is that a linear projection can be read as retrieving stored information scaled by input activations. I define feature recall, show it applies across architectures, and contrast it with the established paradigm of feature combination. I also consider how cases of feature recall might be mechanistically identified. The account gives philosophers a new conceptual tool for understanding deep learning, and points to empirical directions for mechanistic interpretability research.

</details>


### [71] [Belief Without Behavior: Measuring the Translation of Theory of Mind into Coordinated Social Action in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20975)
*Tonglin Yan,Gregoire Sergeant-Perthuis,David Rudrauf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MOSAIC基准，用于联合评估具身智能体的心理理论推理与多模态社会行为协同。实验显示，多数视觉语言模型无法将指定层级的心理状态推理稳定转化为一致的语言、轨迹、视线和表情行为；具有显式心理理论模块的PCM-LLM在全部条件下表现成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测通常将心理理论推理与具身行为能力割裂考察，因而无法衡量模型是否真正能把对他人信念、意图等状态的判断，落实为可协调、可被他者理解的社会行动。论文旨在填补从社会推断到社会行动之间的评测缺口。

Method: 作者构建受控基准MOSAIC：两名具身智能体在多类任务情境中交互，要求同步整合口头陈述、空间移动轨迹、视线方向与面部表情。任务通过系统化改变心理理论阶数约束，检验模型能否生成符合预期推理层级的行为，并能否解释对方信号后作出适当反应。实验评估13个模型，其中包括11个视觉语言模型；每个模型进行200次试验，并开展信号层面的误差分析。

Result: 视觉语言模型整体未能生成与心理理论阶数约束所要求结果一致的行为；即使显式施加不同阶数约束，也没有观察到可靠且与指定推理层级相符的行为变化。误差分析指出两个连续瓶颈：其一，多数模型难以生成方向上连贯的非语言信号；其二，即便产生了信号，模型仍常无法正确解读对方行为并据此调整自身行动。作为结构化参考的PCM-LLM由于具有显式心理理论模块，在所有测试条件下均成功完成任务。

Conclusion: MOSAIC表明，当前视觉语言模型的核心短板不只是心理状态推理本身，还包括将推理结果绑定到多模态动作，以及利用他者动作形成闭环响应的能力。结果支持显式的信念—行动耦合机制是解决此类任务的充分架构要素之一；不过，基于摘要中的证据，其有效性主要限于该基准设定，跨环境泛化与模块消融仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Effective social interaction requires agents to translate mental state inferences into coordinated behavioral signals across verbal and nonverbal channels simultaneously. Yet existing benchmarks evaluate theory of mind (ToM) reasoning and embodied behavior in isolation, leaving unmeasured the gap between social inference and social action. We introduce MOSAIC (Multimodal Orchestration of Social Action, Inference, and Communication), a controlled benchmark in which two embodied agents interact across cooperative and competitive scenarios requiring integration of verbal statements, spatial trajectories, gaze direction, and facial expression under systematically varied ToM constraints. Evaluating 13 models, including 11 VLMs, across 200 trials per model, we find that VLMs fail to produce behaviors consistent with the expected outcomes under ToM-order constraints, and that imposing explicit ToM-order constraints produces no reliable behavioral change aligned with the specified reasoning level. Signal-level analysis reveals two sequential bottlenecks: most models cannot produce directionally coherent nonverbal signals, and even when signals are present, VLM agents fail to interpret others behaviors and react to them. PCM-LLM, included as a structured architectural reference point with an explicit ToM module, succeeds across all conditions, suggesting that explicit belief-action coupling is a sufficient ingredient for this class of tasks.

</details>


### [72] [Don't Solve, Just Compare: Tiny Advisors for Runtime Intervention in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21027)
*Yanze Jiang,Mingxuan Li,Yuhao Wang,Shengfang Zhai,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Comparison-Only Tiny Advisor（COTA）：一种面向长时程大模型智能体的运行时干预框架。它以能力较弱的小型比较器替代专家求解器或任务型批评器，通过比较候选后续轨迹的优劣提供非强制性恢复建议，在三类交互任务和三种智能体上均提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体在推理、工具调用和序列决策中容易累积错误。仅检测失败不足以支持恢复，而现有建设性干预通常依赖额外的强大求解器，或要求批评器具备较强的任务理解与纠错能力，导致计算成本或模型能力门槛较高。论文旨在验证：弱于主智能体的辅助模型，是否仍能提供有效的运行时纠偏。

Method: COTA从同一历史前缀出发采样反事实分支，并以成对监督训练微型比较器，使其判断某一候选延续是否优于主智能体当前提出的延续。系统通过重复比较估计是否值得触发干预；一旦触发，则将较优候选作为非绑定建议返回给原智能体，由原智能体自行重新规划，而非直接替换其行动。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和tau^3-Retail三个基准上，针对三种主智能体形成的九组评测设置中，COTA均带来性能提升，并优于所比较的基线方法。实验表明，辅助比较器不需要具备与主智能体相当的完整任务求解能力，仍可产生有价值的建设性干预。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将运行时恢复从“生成具体纠错方案”转化为“比较候选未来的相对质量”。这一设计降低了辅助模块的能力与成本要求，同时保留主智能体的自主重规划能力。COTA说明，基于反事实分支和成对偏好的轻量比较器，是提升长时程智能体可靠性的可行路径。

Abstract: LLM agents are emerging as an important paradigm for real-world tasks that require reasoning, tool use, and sequential decision-making. As these agents operate over longer horizons, runtime intervention offers a way to improve reliability without retraining the underlying actor. Failure detection alone is insufficient. Effective intervention must also provide a useful direction for recovery. Existing approaches often rely on an expert solver or a critic that generates task-specific corrections, incurring either the cost of another capable solver or the capacity demands of a task-capable critic. We introduce Comparison-Only Tiny Advisor (COTA), a comparison-only framework for constructive runtime intervention. In COTA, a tiny comparator judges whether sampled alternatives lead to better continuations than the actor's proposal, and repeated comparisons determine when intervention is warranted. We train the comparator using pairwise supervision constructed from same-prefix counterfactual branches. Preferred alternatives are returned as non-binding advice, leaving the original actor to replan. Across WebShop, ALFWorld, and tau^3-Retail with three actors, COTA improves all nine evaluation settings and outperforms the compared baselines. These results show that constructive runtime intervention can remain effective even when the auxiliary model has substantially weaker task-solving capability than the actor.

</details>


### [73] [Evaluating Large Language Model Performance on International Maritime Dangerous Goods Code Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21036)
*Alexander Thomas,Hubert P. H. Shum,Darren Nellis,Manli Zhu,Phatpicha Yochum,William Bartle,Daniel Wrightson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DGEval基准，用于系统评估大语言模型解释和应用IMDG Amendment 42-24要求的能力。结果显示，模型在结构化危险货物清单查询方面较有潜力，但在积载、隔离和法规条文回忆等关键任务上仍不可靠，不能脱离人工监督用于安全关键决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海运危险货物运输受IMDG Code复杂且持续更新的法规约束，分类、包装、积载和隔离等环节的误判可能造成严重安全后果。尽管大语言模型正被用于辅助合规判断，目前尚缺乏针对其IMDG法规理解能力的系统性评估。

Method: 构建DGEval基准，基于专家编写的Hazcheck培训平台题目以及危险货物清单（DGL）的结构化查询，形成包含1678道题的数据集，覆盖选择题、开放题、DGL查询和法规识别任务。研究评估了来自6家提供商的13个模型及不同推理配置，并比较了启用网络搜索和使用海事领域微调模型的效果。

Result: 表现最佳的模型在选择题上超过人类从业者基线，但所有模型在积载、隔离和法规信息回忆等操作性和安全关键任务上表现较弱。网络搜索能够提升结构化DGL查询能力，但未消除模型在法规解释和实际操作任务中的不可靠性。

Conclusion: DGEval表明，大语言模型可作为部分IMDG合规任务的辅助工具，尤其适合结合权威来源进行结构化查询；但其当前能力不足以支持无监督的安全关键应用。部署前仍需人工复核、权威法规验证和持续基准测试，DGEval可作为随模型迭代持续开展的安全保障工具。

Abstract: The transport of dangerous goods by sea is a high-consequence activity governed by the International Maritime Dangerous Goods (IMDG) Code, a complex regulatory framework where errors in classification, packaging, stowage, or segregation can result in fire, explosion, toxic release, or loss of life or vessel. Correct compliance requires accurately interpreting hundreds of pages of interacting provisions, updated on a two-year amendment cycle. Practitioners increasingly use Large Language Models (LLMs) as decision-support tools, yet no systematic evaluation exists of whether they can reliably interpret IMDG requirements for safety-critical use.
  This paper introduces DGEval, the first benchmark for evaluating LLM knowledge of IMDG Amendment 42-24. Built from expert-written questions on the NCB Hazcheck e-learning platform and structured lookups from the Dangerous Goods List (DGL), it comprises 1,678 questions across multiple-choice, open-ended, DGL lookup, and regulatory identification tasks. We evaluate 13 models from six providers across multiple thinking configurations, including one maritime domain-specific fine-tuned model, and test the effect of web search.
  Although the best-performing model exceeds the human practitioner baseline on multiple-choice questions, all models are weakest in the operationally safety-critical areas of stowage, segregation, and regulatory recall. These results indicate that LLMs may support compliance tasks, particularly structured DGL lookups with web search, but unreliability in operational areas and regulatory-text recall means human oversight and authoritative source verification remain necessary before deployment in any safety-critical context. DGEval is designed as a safety assurance instrument to be applied continuously as models evolve, not as a settled characterisation of current capability.

</details>


### [74] [Socialized Division and Collaboration: Rethinking Class-Incremental Learning under Optimization Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21044)
*Xinjie Yao,Zhihe Fan,Yunqi Zhu,Jiaqi Zhou,Dengyu Zhao,Zhoupeng Guo,Yan Fan,Guosong Jiang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出社会化分工与协作（SDC）框架，将传统单模型类增量学习改写为多专用模型协同学习，以缓解连续任务间优化方向冲突引发的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一参数空间需持续容纳异质会话的学习动态；当相邻会话的优化目标不兼容时，参数更新会产生破坏性干扰。作者认为，这并非仅是训练策略不足，而是单模型范式在持久优化冲突下的结构性局限。

Method: SDC根据不同会话之间的优化冲突，将学习任务自适应分配给专用模型，并通过协作完成推理。其核心分配机制是基于亥姆霍兹自由能的会话—模型兼容性准则，用于评估会话与现有模型的匹配程度，进而驱动会话分派、模型演化及多模型协同推理。

Result: 摘要未报告具体数据集、评价指标、对比方法或定量实验结果。因此，能够确认的是该工作提出了统一的会话分配、模型演化和协作推理流程，但其相对性能、计算开销、模型数量增长情况及鲁棒性仍需由正文实验验证。

Conclusion: 该研究将持续学习的重点从单体模型内部的抗遗忘机制，转向面向优化冲突的模型分工与协作。其主要贡献是提出以能量兼容性为依据的自适应多模型框架；潜在价值在于适应高度异质的任务流，但也可能面临模型管理、推理成本与分配错误累积等挑战。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning is commonly instantiated as a single-model paradigm, where a unified model sequentially adapts to an unbounded stream of sessions. While effective under mild distributional shifts, this formulation becomes strained when successive sessions induce incompatible optimization directions, leading to destructive interference and catastrophic forgetting. We argue that such forgetting reflects a structural limitation of enforcing heterogeneous learning dynamics within a single parameter space. Motivated by social solidarity theory, we propose Socialized Division and Collaboration (SDC) as a reformulation of continual learning that decomposes session learning across specialized models in response to optimization conflicts, while enabling coordinated collaboration. To support this formulation with a principled allocation mechanism, we introduce an energy-based session-model compatibility criterion grounded in Helmholtz free energy, which guides adaptive session allocation and model evolution under conflicting objectives. This framework integrates session assignment, model evolution, and collaborative inference into a unified pipeline, offering an alternative to monolithic continual learning formulations and highlighting a broader design principle for learning under persistent optimization conflicts.

</details>


### [75] [The Cost of a Physics Prior Is Bounded by the Ablation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21059)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出：形状约束的“精度代价”并非仅由先验本身决定，而主要受未受约束特征及验证切分协议影响。基于消融假设类包含于约束假设类，作者证明对任意风险泛函均有0≤P≤D，并据此提出实验可辨识性筛查与报告分辨率校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形状约束与物理先验学习通常将性能下降归因于先验约束，但不同验证协议、空间泛化设置和自由特征可能显著改变该下降幅度。论文旨在区分“先验代价”与“数据切分及特征配置造成的表观代价”，避免将不可迁移的实验结果误解为先验固有性质。

Method: 定义P为将假设类限制为在特征集合S上满足形状约束后的超额风险，D为直接忽略S的消融模型超额风险。由于任一对x_j恒定的函数同时满足非递减与非递增约束，消融类包含于约束类，因此得到0≤P≤D，无需凸性、光滑性或可实现性条件。实证上，作者在含三个有序类别、26,681个样本的数据任务中，对四个驱动变量施加硬单调约束，保留坐标特征自由，并比较独立同分布重采样与不同尺度空间分块验证。进一步以约束模型不应劣于其自身消融模型作为符号检验，并通过包含关系的反转估计评测流程的加性分辨率下限。

Result: 空间分块下，仅使用坐标即可恢复完整模型92.9%的宏平均F1，使D由0.1288降至0.0427。相同单调先验在坐标未受约束的“屏蔽”配置下代价为0.0473，而移除该屏蔽后为0.3470，比例为7.3。将空间块尺度从1增至10度时，D从0.0942降至0.0050，说明该量强烈依赖验证协议，部分配置无法事前区分。318次比较得到0.0220宏平均F1的自校准分辨率下限，低于该阈值的代价估计不具可解释性。未施加约束时的先验违背率为0.48至0.49，表明先验合规程度与性能代价并不等价。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是一个普适的假设类嵌套界：约束代价P必不超过忽略受约束特征的代价D。该结论将形状约束评估从“先验是否昂贵”转向“实验协议是否能识别该代价”。实践中，应在训练约束模型前先执行两次拟合的筛查，报告验证切分、自由特征和评测分辨率，并避免将低于流程分辨率或高度依赖协议的差异解释为先验的固有成本。

Abstract: Shape-constrained and physics-informed learning reports an accuracy cost of enforcing a prior and treats it as a property of the prior. We show it is mostly a property of the free features and the validation split. Let P be the excess risk of restricting a hypothesis class to functions with a shape constraint on features S, and D the excess risk of the ablated model that ignores S. Because a function constant in x_j is both non-decreasing and non-increasing in x_j, the ablated class is contained in the constrained class, so 0 <= P <= D for every risk functional, with no convexity, smoothness, or realizability assumption. Empirically the bound is a sign test: a constrained model must never be beaten by its own ablation. We instantiate it on an ordinal wildfire-severity task (N = 26,681, K = 3) with hard monotone constraints on four meteorological drivers, coordinates left free, and a validation ladder from i.i.d. resampling to 2-degree spatial blocking. Coordinates act as a shield: alone they recover 92.9% of the full model's macro-F1 under spatial blocking, collapsing D from 0.1288 to 0.0427; the same prior costs 0.0473 shielded and 0.3470 unshielded, a ratio of 7.3 with identical physics. Because D is protocol-dependent it does not transfer: coarsening blocks from 1 to 10 degrees drives D from 0.0942 to 0.0050, leaving two configurations unidentifiable a priori. Inversions of the certified nesting bound the pipeline's additive resolution: over 318 comparisons they give a self-calibrating floor of 0.0220 macro-F1, below which no reported price is interpretable, including four cells in our own headline grid. Cost and compliance are independent: the unconstrained model violates the prior at rate 0.48-0.49 while enforcing it costs 0.0473. We give a two-fit screen that rejects unidentifiable experiments before a constrained model is trained.

</details>


### [76] [CellPath-Bench: A Multidimensional Benchmark for Whole-Slide Cellular Representations in Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21060)
*Bokai Zhao,Yiyang Zhang,Hanqing Chao,Yawei Ma,Long Bai,Tai Ma,Minfeng Xu,Ming Song,Tianzi Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CellPath-Bench，用于系统评估病理基础模型在全切片图像特征中编码细胞类型信息的能力，以及该信息跨切片、数据集和器官的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型评测通常难以在细胞分辨率下诊断模型表征：一是无法量化核位置特征相较于图块平均特征对细胞类型识别的增益；二是缺少对细胞类型可解码性跨域泛化能力的统一评估。因此，需要一个基于空间配准数据、可比较且标准化的细胞级基准。

Method: 作者从52个候选Xenium数据集中完成质控，构建包含25张H&E与Xenium空间配准组织切片的数据面板，覆盖11个器官和7,079,283个细胞，并建立细粒度与粗粒度细胞分类体系。对于冻结的病理基础模型，基准在配准后的细胞核坐标处采样全切片特征图，以标准化多类别线性探针评估细胞类型。CRA指标量化核锚定特征相对图块均值池化的同切片优势；CRT指标衡量细胞类型信息在不同切片、数据集和器官之间的迁移泛化。实验覆盖30个病理专用或通用基础模型，在空间读取方式、放大倍率、分类粒度和评估协议等条件下共进行304,920次运行。

Result: 不同模型在细胞类型可解码性及其跨域泛化能力上存在显著差异。模型的优势并非单一维度：有些模型更擅长在细胞核位置保留可区分的细胞信息，有些则在跨切片、跨数据集或跨器官转移中表现更稳健，因而形成不同的多维能力画像。

Conclusion: CellPath-Bench提供了一个面向冻结病理基础模型的标准化细胞级审计框架，可用于比较模型是否真正保留了细胞类型相关信息，以及这些信息能否可靠迁移至新组织条件。该基准有助于将病理基础模型评价从通用下游任务性能推进到细胞表征质量与泛化能力的细粒度诊断。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) are increasingly used as general-purpose backbones, yet existing benchmarks cannot systematically diagnose their whole-slide cellular representation capabilities, including the decodability of cell-type information and the transferability of such information across tissue sections, datasets, and anatomical organs. We introduce CellPath-Bench, a cellular-resolution benchmark that evaluates frozen PFMs themselves. Following quality control of 52 candidate Xenium datasets, we construct a panel of 25 spatially aligned H\&E--Xenium tissue sections spanning 11 organs and 7,079,283 cells, harmonized into fine- and coarse-grained taxonomies. CellPath-Bench samples frozen WSI feature maps at registered nuclear coordinates and evaluates them using standardized multiclass linear probes. Cell Representation Advantage (CRA) measures the within-section advantage of nucleus-anchored representations over patch-level mean pooling, while Cell Representation Transferability (CRT) characterizes the generalization of cell-type decodability across tissue sections, datasets, and organs. We benchmark 30 pathology-specific and general-purpose foundation models through 304,920 runs across spatial readouts, magnifications, taxonomic granularities, and evaluation protocols. The results reveal substantial model-dependent differences in cell-type decodability and its cross-domain generalization, yielding distinct multidimensional capability profiles. CellPath-Bench provides a standardized framework for auditing cellular information in frozen PFM representations.

</details>


### [77] [Can Legal AI Know When It Is Wrong? And Do Students Know When It Is?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21089)
*Angel Mary John,Vipin Kumar Singh,Jerrin Thomas Panachakel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文审计了三类大语言模型在合同法任务中的高置信错误，并调查法学生对模型幻觉的核验行为与培训缺口。核心发现是：模型会以极高确信度给出错误法律结论，且人类核验通常在发现问题后才被动启动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估将大语言模型用于司法与法律研究时的可靠性风险，特别关注模型未能跟进法律修订、错误援引既有规则，以及用户因模型自信表达而可能降低审查强度的问题。

Method: 第一阶段构建包含60个合同法案例的测试集，比较ChatGPT、Meta AI和Perplexity AI的裁断正确性及1—10分自评置信度；以置信度不低于9分的错误答案占比定义高置信错误率（HCER）。第二阶段对380名法学生开展问卷，比较有无接触虚构引文经历的学生在核验行为上的差异，并统计其对风险后果和伦理培训的认知。

Result: 模型普遍难以正确处理法律更新。Meta AI的HCER最高，为31.7%，平均置信度为9.1/10；Perplexity AI为15.0%；ChatGPT为6.7%。问卷显示，曾遇到虚构引文的学生核验得分更高（4.2/5），未遇到者较低（2.8/5）。81.6%的受访者知道提交虚构案例可能产生严重程序后果，但71.1%未接受正式的伦理AI使用培训。

Conclusion: 论文认为，法律场景中的关键风险并非仅是模型出错，而是“高置信错误”造成的误导性权威感。建议在法律教育中采用对抗式法律检索训练，并在系统层面部署基于可追溯权威来源的验证架构。需注意，摘要中的“先例过拟合”机制仍属假设；案例集构成、正确答案标注流程、模型版本与提示词控制、统计显著性及问卷抽样代表性均需在全文中进一步验证。

Abstract: Integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) into the Indian judiciary promises access to justice but introduces severe risks. We identify the 'inertia of confidence'--an overconfidence phenomenon analogous to the Dunning-Kruger effect where LLMs provide incorrect legal verdicts with near-maximum confidence, driven by a hypothesized 'precedent overfitting' bias. Phase I of our socio-technical audit tested ChatGPT (GPT-5.2), Meta AI, and Perplexity AI on a 60-case battery regarding the Indian Contract Act, 1872, and the shift toward statutory enforcement of specific performance. We introduce the High-Confidence Error Rate (HCER) to quantify incorrect verdicts delivered with dangerous certainty (>= 9 on a 1-10 scale). All models struggled with statutory updates. Meta AI proved most vulnerable (31.7% HCER), frequently misapplying pre-amendment rules with a 9.1/10 mean confidence, followed by Perplexity (15.0%) and ChatGPT (6.7%).
  Phase II investigated human vulnerability to this overconfidence via a survey of Indian law students (N=380). Verification often functions as a reactive adaptation to machine hallucinations: students encountering fabricated citations reported higher verification scores (4.2/5) than those with no such encounters (2.8/5). Furthermore, while 81.6% knew submitting hallucinated cases can lead to contempt-of-court, 71.1% received no formal training on ethical AI use. We propose shifting toward adversarial legal research pedagogy and implementing source-grounded verification architectures to prevent systemic professional negligence.

</details>


### [78] [When Trust Meets Truth: Trust-Truth Separability in LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21097)
*Xin Sun,Di Wu,Yuchen Guo,Jiahuan Pei,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究指出，LLM 评审器输出的“信任度”和“真实性”并非彼此独立：在问答评测中，两类判断高度耦合，且仅改变答案来源标签就会同时改变信任评分与真伪判定。因此，不应将信任评分视为支持真实性判断的独立证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-Judge 常被用于生成可信度、可靠性、事实性等多维评价，并可能默认这些维度提供相互独立的证据。论文检验这一关键假设，聚焦“信任评分”与“二元真伪分类”之间是否存在足够的判断分离。

Method: 作者在答案正确性受控的问答数据上，对比 LLM 评审器的信任评分与真伪判定的一致程度，并以人类行为参考作为基线。随后进行来源线索压力测试：保持问答内容完全不变，仅将来源归因为人类或 AI，考察来源标签对信任分数、真伪结论及基于 logits 推导的“判为正确”的概率之影响。

Result: LLM 评审器的信任评分与真伪裁决之间的对齐程度高于人类行为参考，表明模型对“信任”和“真实”的表征或决策边界分离较弱。更重要的是，来源归因的变化会系统性地同时改变信任判断、真伪判断和正确侧概率，即使问答文本本身没有变化。

Conclusion: 现有 LLM-as-Judge 协议中，信任评分与真实性判断存在显著依赖和共同的来源偏差，不能被当作相互独立的评测维度或证据来源。未来评测应显式检验维度独立性、控制来源线索，并避免对多个高度耦合指标进行重复证据解释。

Abstract: LLM-as-Judge systems can produce multi-dimensional evaluations, such as trustworthiness, reliability, and factuality, and these outputs are often interpreted as independent evidence. We test this assumption for a common pair of judgments: trust scoring and binary truth classification. On correctness-controlled QA, LLM judges align trust scores with truth verdicts more tightly than human behavioral reference, suggesting weaker separations between trust and truth judgment. We then apply stress tests by changing only source cues of identical QA between Human and AI. Source attribution shifts not only trust scores but also truth verdicts and logit-derived correct-side probabilities. Results show that current LLM-as-Judge protocols should not treat trust scores as independent evidence for truth judgments.

</details>


### [79] [ReFrame: Evidence-Guided Test-Time Safety Alignment in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21100)
*Wenzheng Jiang,Xuankun Rong,Yuanzhao Zhai,Dawei Feng,Huaimin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ReFrame，一种无需训练、无需访问内部状态的多模态测试时安全对齐框架，通过风险与效用证据生成、提示重写及图像路由，提升多模态大模型的安全性并保持任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态安全对齐方法通常依赖模型再训练或内部状态检查，难以应用于已部署的闭源模型；同时，跨模态安全绕过、安全意识不足和过度拒答等问题仍未充分解决。论文进一步指出，效用主导和推理惯性是导致模型忽略潜在风险或沿不安全推理路径运行的关键障碍。

Method: ReFrame采用两个代理共享的轻量级本地多模态模型：证据生成代理构造互补的风险证据与效用证据；重写与路由代理据此生成安全代理提示，并决定图像是否及如何路由至下游多模态大模型。整个过程无需训练下游模型，也不访问其内部信息。

Result: 在多个多模态大模型和基准上的实验表明，ReFrame能够提升安全绕过防御能力和安全意识，降低过度敏感拒答，同时较好保持多模态任务效用。

Conclusion: 通过在调用下游模型前重构输入并进行图像路由，ReFrame为闭源多模态大模型提供了一种实用的训练无关测试时安全对齐方案，在安全性与可用性之间取得了较好的平衡。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend model capabilities beyond text, they also make safety alignment increasingly challenging. Multimodal safety alignment methods must address cross-modal jailbreaks, safety-awareness failures, and over-sensitive refusals. However, existing methods often rely on retraining or internal-state inspection, limiting their applicability to deployed closed-source MLLMs and motivating test-time safety alignment. We analyze this setting and identify two key obstacles, utility dominance and reasoning inertia, which cause models to overlook latent risks or follow malicious reasoning trajectories. Guided by these insights, we propose ReFrame, a training-free multimodal input reframing framework where two agents share a lightweight locally deployed MLLM: the evidence-generation agent constructs complementary risk and utility evidence, and the rewrite-and-routing agent converts it into a safe proxy prompt and image-routing decision before calling the downstream MLLM, without modifying it or accessing its internal information. Experiments across multiple MLLMs and benchmarks show that ReFrame improves jailbreak defense, safety awareness, and oversensitivity reduction while preserving multimodal utility.

</details>


### [80] [Root cause analysis via difference graph discovery from linear time-series data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21117)
*Anouk Ruer,Timothée Loranchet,Daria Bystrova,Charles K. Assaad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将复杂动态系统中的异常根因分析建模为“差异图发现”问题：比较正常与异常两个时间序列机制，定位因果系数发生变化、且可能表面上违背常见效果模式的根因变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异常检测通常只能指出哪些变量异常，难以解释导致异常的底层机制。对于具有时序依赖和因果传播的系统，需要识别正常机制与异常机制之间发生变化的因果关系，从而更准确地定位根因。

Method: 作者采用线性离散时间动态结构因果模型形式化问题，将原本用于比较两个独立总体差异图的方法迁移到时间序列场景，其中两个总体分别对应正常状态与异常状态。重点识别跨状态发生变化的因果系数，并将相关变量视为效应反常型根因候选。

Result: 在模拟数据上，所提适配方法被用于评估差异因果结构识别能力；在IT监控和重症监护监测等真实数据上，方法展示了定位异常相关因果机制的实际可行性。摘要未给出具体的定量指标、方法名称或不同方法之间的性能比较。

Conclusion: 差异图发现为时序系统根因分析提供了因果机制层面的解决框架。通过比较正常与异常状态下的动态因果关系，可辅助定位引发异常行为的机制变化，而不仅是识别观测值异常的变量。

Abstract: Root cause analysis aims to identify the mechanisms responsible for anomalies in complex dynamical systems. In this paper, we study root cause analysis in linear time-series through the lens of difference graph discovery. We focus on effect-defying root causes, corresponding to variables whose causal coefficients change between a normal and an anomalous regime. We formalize this problem using linear discrete-time dynamic structural causal models and adapt several methods originally introduced for discovering difference graphs between two populations to the time-series setting, where the two populations are replaced by a normal and an anomalous regime. We first evaluate the proposed approaches on simulated data, and then demonstrate their practical relevance on real-world datasets from IT monitoring and intensive care monitoring. Our results show how difference graph discovery can help localize causal mechanisms responsible for anomalous behavior.

</details>


### [81] [From Attention Masks to Inert Zero-Vector Tokens: OAttention and O-Closure for Token Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21174)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“支持耦合注意力”（OAttention）及其配套 OTransformer 组件，为令牌引入由隐藏向量范数决定的存在系数，使零向量令牌在注意力与组合模块中具有严格的“非参与”语义。实验显示，在克隆的预训练 TabPFN v3 回归器上零微调替换后，性能变化很小，但结论仅限于所测设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统注意力掩码只能限制查询—源令牌对之间的交互，不能让令牌自身携带“未参与计算”的状态。因此，向序列插入零令牌时，普通注意力及后续模块通常无法保证输出严格不变。论文旨在构造一种由表示本身决定令牌参与度的机制，并使其在注意力、归一化、前馈与残差组合中保持闭包。

Method: 对每个令牌隐藏载体 h_i 定义存在系数 p_i=||h_i||^2/(τ+||h_i||^2)。在 OAttention 中，p_i 对接收端输出进行门控；源端 p_j 同时进入注意力分子和归一化配分，从而保持标准打分、可见性关系、指数竞争及值聚合的结构。随后定义 OFFN、ONorm、OInject 与按存在度加权的 OStandardize，并以 O-Closure 定律 M(H⊕0)=M(H)⊕0 为目标，通过残差和组合构建 OTransformer。作者以契约测试验证零元、空支持集、空接收端及插入零令牌等性质，并在预训练模型改造实验和双模块消融中评估。

Result: 理论与契约测试表明，零向量令牌可作为严格零元：零接收端输出为零，零源令牌不影响其他令牌，自注意力插入与空支持集情形满足预期。GPU 实验中，在 18 个配对的数据集—随机种子案例上，对克隆的预训练 TabPFN v3 回归器进行零微调改造后，校准隐藏载体的 OAttention 平均 RMSE 变化为 +0.088%，Full-O 为 +0.177%。两模块消融显示，仅替换 OAttention 时，普通宿主组件不能持续保持 NULL 状态；采用完整 OTransformer 路径时可维持该性质。

Conclusion: 该工作核心贡献是将令牌级存在度同时用于发射信息与参与共享统计，进而实现零令牌插入不变性和组合闭包。实证结果支持其与既有活跃路径具有较高兼容性，且完整组件链对保持 NULL 语义是必要的。但论文并未证明性能普遍无损、对任意既有网络都自动闭包、训练过程会主动形成零表示，亦未给出缺失值的一般性语义解释。

Abstract: Attention masks are relation-level controls: they specify which query--source pairs may interact. They do not provide a representation-carried token state that is non-participating at the attention boundary. We assign each token hidden carrier \(h_i\) an active-presence coefficient \(p_i=\lVert h_i\rVert^2/(τ+\lVert h_i\rVert^2)\). The same coefficient has two roles: it gates information emitted by token \(i\), and it determines the mass with which token \(i\) enters computations shared with other tokens.
  OAttention is the support-coupled attention realization of this rule. It gates the receiver output by \(p_i\) and weights source \(j\) by \(p_j\) in both the attention numerator and partition, while retaining the standard score, visibility relation, exponential competition, and value aggregation. This makes the zero-vector token a zero element and yields exact null-receiver, null-source insertion, self-attention insertion, and empty-support properties. The same token-level presence gives local O-components (OFFN, ONorm, and OInject), presence-weighted OStandardize, the O-Closure law \(M(H\oplus0)=M(H)\oplus0\), and an OTransformer by residual and compositional closure.
  The canonical operator is checked by contract tests and a GPU evaluation. In a zero-fine-tuning retrofit of a cloned pretrained TabPFN v3 regressor, calibrated hidden-carrier OAttention and Full-O variants change mean RMSE by $+0.088\%$ and $+0.177\%$, respectively, over 18 matched dataset--seed cases. A two-block ablation shows that OAttention alone does not preserve a NULL state through ordinary host components, whereas the OTransformer path does. These are scoped tests of exactness, active-path compatibility, and compositional necessity; they do not establish universal no-loss, arbitrary-host closure, learned attraction to the origin, or a general semantics for missing values.

</details>


### [82] [SENTRY: Deterministic, Intelligent Risk Assessment for IT Change Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21203)
*Daniel Arulpragasam,Christer Henrysson,Ella Ly,Deepika Anbalagan,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SENTRY：一个用于大型金融机构技术变更风险评估的平台。它以确定性的XGBoost模型结合混合检索增强机制替代主观问卷评分，在保持可审计性与可解释性的同时提升高风险变更识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自报式风险问卷具有主观性强、容易被策略性填写、难以区分常规变更与后续易引发重大技术事件的变更等缺陷。受监管环境要求风险评估结果准确、一致且可审计，因此需要一种可复现、可解释的数据驱动方法。

Method: SENTRY融合结构化运维元数据、应用依赖图和历史事件记录，并对历史变更请求文本执行语义检索与词法检索相结合的混合检索。检索得到的非结构化风险信号被压缩为单一标量特征，再与其他特征共同输入梯度提升决策树模型。该设计避免让生成式模块直接参与最终决策，从而维持推理确定性；同时利用SHAP值提供逐样本特征归因。

Result: 在企业规模的变更数据上，SENTRY取得0.87的ROC AUC和85%的总体准确率。相较现有流程，系统对高风险变更的检出率约为原来的3.25倍，表明混合检索提取的文本风险信号能够补充结构化数据中的信息缺口。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在高审计要求的技术变更管理中，可将检索增强能力限制为特征构造环节，并以确定性树模型承担最终评分，从而在预测性能、可审计性和解释性之间取得平衡。其核心价值不在于用生成模型直接自动决策，而在于将非结构化历史经验转化为可验证的风险特征。

Abstract: Technology change management in large financial institutions depends on risk assessments that are accurate, consistent, and auditable. In practice, many institutions still rely on self-reported questionnaires. Those questionnaires are subjective, easy to game, and poor at separating routine changes from the ones that later trigger major incidents. This paper presents SENTRY, a risk assessment platform that replaces questionnaire-based scoring with a deterministic machine learning pipeline built from gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost) and hybrid retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The system combines structured operational metadata, application dependency graphs, and historical incident records with a hybrid semantic and lexical search over historical change requests. The retrieval step captures the risk signal in unstructured change request text, then compresses that signal into a single scalar feature before model inference. That design keeps the model deterministic and preserves per-prediction explainability via SHAP values. Evaluated on enterprise-scale change data, SENTRY achieves a ROC AUC of 0.87 and 85% overall accuracy, and it detects high-risk changes at roughly 3.25 times the rate of the existing process. We close by examining the architectural trade-offs behind this design and what they imply for the use of machine learning in regulated change management.

</details>


### [83] [Personalized Privacy Control in LLMs via Attention Head Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21209)
*Junseok Kim,Nakyeong Yang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“个性化隐私”概念与P3Bench基准，用于评估大语言模型是否能依据不同用户的披露偏好处理信息；并提出Repair推理时干预方法，以提升模型对个性化隐私策略的遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有上下文隐私研究主要依据场景规范判断信息是否应披露，但同一场景中不同用户对信息披露的容忍边界可能不同。随着具备工具调用与数据访问能力的智能体模型普及，仅依赖通用上下文规则不足以保障用户特定的隐私偏好。

Method: 作者将用户特定的信息披露偏好纳入隐私控制，定义个性化隐私策略；构建P3Bench，在上下文隐私策略基础上扩展个体化披露规则。随后评估提示词式策略控制的有效性，并提出Repair：一种在推理阶段对注意力头进行干预的方法，使模型输出更接近给定策略要求。

Result: 实验表明，仅通过提示词提供策略难以稳定约束模型遵循个性化隐私规则。Qwen2.5-7B与Gemma3-4B的平均策略忽略率分别为51.25%和74.28%。Repair能够显著减少模型未遵守给定用户隐私策略的情形，提高策略一致性。

Conclusion: 个性化隐私是对传统上下文隐私控制的重要补充。P3Bench为该问题提供了标准化评测基础，而Repair显示推理时模型内部干预可有效改善个体化隐私策略遵循；摘要未提供其具体实现细节、跨模型泛化性及对正常回答质量影响等信息，仍需结合全文进一步验证。

Abstract: The rise of agentic AI enables LLMs to access diverse user data, raising critical privacy concerns. Prior work on contextual privacy studies whether LLMs regulate information disclosure according to context-dependent norms. However, acceptable disclosure boundaries may vary across users even within the same context. To address this limitation, we introduce \textit{personalized privacy}, which incorporates user-specific disclosure preferences into privacy control. We further present P3Bench~(\textbf{P}ersonalized \textbf{P}rivacy \textbf{P}reservation \textbf{Bench}mark), a novel benchmark extending contextual privacy policies with personalized disclosure policies. Experiments show that prompt-based policies fail to reliably enforce personalized privacy policies, with Qwen2.5-7B and Gemma3-4B showing average policy ignorance ratios of 51.25\% and 74.28\%, respectively. Finally, to address this problem, we propose \textsc{Repair}, a robust inference-time attention head intervention method that adjusts disclosure behavior toward policy-consistent responses. Our method significantly improves adherence to user-specific privacy preferences by reducing cases where the model fails to follow the given policy.

</details>


### [84] [Enhancing LLMs in Predictive Political QA with Semi-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21218)
*Yinan Liu,Zihan Zhou,Zichun Jin,Xinyu Wang,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Predictive political question answering (QA), such as predicting how a political actor will vote, goes beyond factual lookup. External political resources offer rich historical evidence, but rarely contain the answer itself. Existing LLM augmentation methods, including actor-profile-based simulation and knowledge graph evidence injection, improve political reasoning but largely treat external resources as knowledge-based evidence, leaving prediction-relevant signals under-modeled. We identify two complementary signals for predictive political QA: actor stances that capture issue-specific preferences, and high-order structure signals that capture indirect dependencies among political actors. We propose PSL, a dual-view framework that converts semi-structured political records into inference-oriented evidence for LLMs. PSL extracts stance signals from question-relevant actor records in a semantic view, and learns structure-aware actor representations from an actor interaction graph in a vector view. Across three real-world datasets and multiple LLMs, PSL consistently outperforms baselines, with ablations confirming the complementary gains of stance and structure signals.

</details>


### [85] [Ontology-supported AI Model and Dataset Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21224)
*Jan Novacek,Ali Ahari,Tobias Müller,Sebastian Reiter,Alexander Viehl,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个面向工业场景的AI模型交换平台，通过本体驱动的语义表示来统一管理、追踪、发现和交换AI模型、数据集及其关联资产，以减少跨组织协作中的语义缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI研发与应用过程中会产生大量模型、数据集及相关元数据资产。不同组织采用异构描述方式，导致资产的可追溯性、可管理性、可发现性和可比性不足。论文旨在明确高效交换与管理这些资产所需的信息和语义基础。

Method: 作者构建AI模型交换平台，支持模型和数据集的使用、交换与分析；平台引入领域本体，对资产类型、属性、关系及上下文进行形式化描述，以建立共享语义并支撑资产检索、追踪和管理。论文还通过实时关键系统场景的用例说明平台工作方式。

Result: 摘要表明，该平台能够将模型、数据集及相关资产纳入统一的语义化管理框架，并借助本体提升各方对资产描述和交换要求的一致理解。用例用于展示该方案在实时关键系统环境下的实用性，但摘要未给出量化性能指标或与基线方案的对比结果。

Conclusion: 该研究的核心贡献是将本体技术整合到AI资产交换平台中，为工业环境中的AI资产互操作、溯源、发现和管理提供语义基础。其实际效果仍需结合全文中的本体覆盖范围、平台实现细节、互操作标准及实验评估进一步判断。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a great deal of research into improving AI methods and their application. The main focus is on tracking progress, enabling transparent comparisons, and fostering a more profound understanding of AI. In that process, different organizations generate and use plenty of assets that need to be tracked, traced and managed. Moreover, it is important to discover assets relevant for the task at hand. This paper presents research aiming to contribute to answering the question of what is required to exchange and manage AI models and related assets effectively without semantic gaps in an industrial context. We introduce a platform for AI model exchange, which facilitates the usage, exchange, and analysis of AI models and datasets. The platform incorporates an ontology that can foster a more profound common understanding of what is required in these tasks and help tackle the issues mentioned above. Finally, we elucidate the utility of the platform through the illustration of a use case in the context of real-time critical systems.

</details>


### [86] [Fine-Grain GPU Parallelization of the Generalized Partition Crossover for Large-Scale Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21233)
*Swetha Varadarajan,Darrell Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向大规模旅行商问题的广义分区交叉算子分区阶段GPU细粒度实现，将原本包含不规则访问、图遍历与顺序依赖的交叉过程改写为图并行计算，在1万至200万城市实例上相对朴素串行CPU实现获得48至625倍加速，并降低内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗传算法旅行商求解器的交叉算子虽具有较强效果，但难以适配大规模并行硬件；已有GPU方法大多仅并行化种群维度，因而可处理的问题规模有限。论文旨在挖掘算子内部并行性，突破大规模实例中交叉阶段的性能与存储瓶颈。

Method: 作者将GPX的分区阶段重构为图并行流程：采用利于合并访存的数据布局；通过幽灵节点变换处理高连接度结构；使用CUDA并行执行双亲回路并集构造、度为4顶点拆分、公共边删除，以及重组连通分量识别。核心技术包括面向GPU的内存组织和连通分量分析，以降低不规则图操作带来的执行效率损失。

Result: 实验覆盖1万、至多200万城市的实例。GPU分区实现相对于朴素串行CPU基线达到48倍至625倍加速，同时显著减少内存额外开销。结果表明，该实现能够有效扩展GPX分区阶段至超大规模问题。

Conclusion: 论文证明遗传算法旅行商求解器不应仅依赖种群级并行，还可通过将复杂交叉算子转化为适合GPU执行的图并行原语来获得显著可扩展性。需注意，摘要比较对象为朴素串行CPU实现，未说明与高优化多线程CPU实现或端到端完整求解器的对比，因此整体求解收益仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is one of the most extensively studied NP-hard optimization problems. Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based solvers, such as the Edge Assembly Crossover (EAX), achieve state-of-the-art performance on many benchmark instances. However, the scalability of these approaches in massively parallel architectures remains limited because crossover operations involve irregular memory access patterns, graph traversals, and sequential dependencies. Existing GPU-based TSP solvers primarily exploit population-level parallelism and are limited to relatively small problem sizes. This work presents a fine-grain GPU implementation of the partition phase of the Generalized Partition Crossover (GPX) operator for large-scale TSP instances. The proposed approach reformulates GPX partitioning as a graph-parallel problem using coalesced memory layouts, ghost-node transformations, and connected-component analysis. The im- plementation parallelizes the union of parent tours, the splitting of degree- four vertices, the deletion of common edges, and the identification of recombining components using CUDA. Experimental results on instances ranging from 10,000 to 2 million cities demonstrate substantial acceleration over a naive sequential CPU imple- mentation. The proposed GPU partitioning achieves speedups between 48x and 625x while significantly reducing memory overhead. The re- sults demonstrate that operator-level parallelism can substantially im- prove the scalability of GA-based TSP solvers on modern many-core architectures.

</details>


### [87] [CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21278)
*Chengxiao Wang,Enyi Jiang,Xiaojing Liao,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR，一种通过隐藏状态门控连续调节安全低秩适配器强度的方法，在降低不当输出风险的同时，较好保留模型在正常任务上的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局安全微调可能同时改变模型对正常与高风险输入的响应，导致实用性下降。研究旨在实现更有条件、更精细的安全适配，缓解安全性与实用性之间的权衡。

Method: CLEAR在冻结的基础模型上引入轻量级隐藏状态门控模块，根据输入表征连续控制安全低秩适配器的激活强度，而非对所有输入统一施加安全调整。研究使用安全性与实用性基准，并与监督微调（SFT）及标准LoRA进行比较。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上，CLEAR将HarmBench的攻击成功率从32.3%降至0.5%，同时保留大部分基础模型实用性；在GSM8K上，相较全局应用的SFT或LoRA，准确率最高提升7.1个百分点。

Conclusion: CLEAR能够以条件化、连续化的方式执行安全适配，在显著提升安全鲁棒性的同时减少对正常任务性能的损害，表明其是改善大语言模型安全—实用性权衡的有效机制。

Abstract: Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [88] [AUSO: Action-Level Unified Skill Optimization from Internalization to Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21292)
*Huizu Lin,Chengkai Huang,Tianqi Gao,Tao Huang,Daijiao Liu,Tongxin Li,Xiaoyan Sun,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AUSO（动作级统一技能优化），以渐进式、动作感知的强化学习框架统一技能学习与技能调用。其核心是在训练早期吸收教师技能指导，中期强化基于环境结果的自主能力，后期按动作层面评估技能是否真正有益，从而提升任务表现与分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能增强智能体通常将技能置于模型外部、完全内化技能，或依赖带噪声的任务级成功率在不同目标间选择。这些方案割裂了训练过程，并默认同一轨迹中的所有动作具有相同的技能价值；但实际上，技能提示可能改善部分决策，也可能干扰另一些决策。因此需要一种能够描述技能从监督信号到可选择决策知识之生命周期的方法。

Method: AUSO以强化学习为贯穿各阶段的共同优化骨架。训练初期，联合教师指导与环境回报，使策略获得基础技能且保持任务导向；随后提高结果驱动的策略优化权重，以巩固自主求解能力；在策略成熟阶段，对每个采样动作分别在技能条件和无技能条件下进行评估，构造动作级信息信号，并与轨迹结果优势相结合。由此，受技能帮助的动作获得更强更新，而受技能干扰的动作被抑制。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA三个基准上，AUSO相较有竞争力的基线方法取得了稳定的性能提升，并表现出更好的分布外泛化能力。摘要未给出具体数值、消融实验细节或计算开销。

Conclusion: 该工作将技能视为会随策略能力演进而改变作用的知识来源，并通过动作级收益评估避免了对技能的统一、静态依赖。AUSO的主要贡献在于将技能获取、能力形成和选择性调用纳入连续的强化学习过程，使技能仅在能够改善当前决策时产生更大影响。

Abstract: Skills play different roles as an agent's policy evolves: they should first provide learnable knowledge, then support capability formation, and finally be invoked only when they improve individual decisions. Existing methods rarely model this lifecycle. They either keep skills outside the model, fully internalize them, or select among internalization and utilization objectives through noisy task-level success rates. Such designs fragment training and assign uniform importance to actions within the same trajectory, even though skill guidance may help some decisions while distracting others. To solve these problems, we introduce AUSO (Action-level Unified Skill Optimization), which unifies skill learning and skill use through a progressive, action-aware optimization process. At the beginning of training, AUSO jointly learns from teacher guidance and environmental outcomes, enabling the policy to acquire foundational skills without losing task-oriented feedback. It subsequently emphasizes outcome-based policy optimization to consolidate autonomous problem-solving ability. As the policy matures, AUSO evaluates each sampled action under both skill-conditioned and skill-free contexts. The resulting action-level information signal is coupled with the trajectory outcome advantage, allowing beneficial skill-sensitive actions to receive stronger updates and harmful ones to be suppressed. Therefore, skills gradually transition from an external source of supervision into decision knowledge whose utilization is adapted to its action-level benefit, while reinforcement learning remains the shared backbone across all stages. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA show that AUSO consistently improves agent performance and out-of-distribution generalization over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [89] [From Regulation to Implementation: A Critical Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Regulatory Compliance in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21317)
*Adriana Watson,Marco Bücheler,Grant Richards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: The European Union (EU) has emerged as a leading regulatory body in the development of sustainability and privacy regulations. While new regulation requirements vary, many include a documentation artifact to ensure compliance. Notably, the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) introduces Digital Product Passports (DPPs) for life cycle transparency, while the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) mandates Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIAs) to mitigate privacy risks. Creating these compliance artifacts, however, is challenging. Industrial data, which often exists in heterogeneous formats and is scattered across company and supplier systems, is required for DPPs and can be difficult to extract into compliant DPP formatting. Furthermore, DPIA documents require interdisciplinary expertise and follow no standardized format, making development difficult for novel systems. To address the particular complexity of compliance artifact creation for both regulations, researchers have proposed the use of LLMs in the generation process; however, the impact of the aforementioned problems on the output of these systems is largely unaddressed. This work investigates the existing research gap by exploring how data extraction instructions and regulatory vagueness impact the quality and consistency of LLM-produced compliance artifacts. The resulting artifacts are evaluated by benchmarking different models against manually created ground-truth schemas. The results reveal that less strict guidelines, such as DPIA formatting, require higher context prompts to maintain consistency and completeness. Stricter guidelines, such as formatting for Digital Battery Passports (DBP), result in consistent results regardless of prompt context, but may lead to more hallucinations in the output

</details>


### [90] [Unified Branch-and-Bound Search for the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem on Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21319)
*Jingtao Tang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将斯坦纳旅行商问题扩展到凸集图（GCS）上，并提出统一的分支定界搜索框架，在无限候选轨迹空间中寻找低成本闭合路径；方法可同时处理可选中转、重复访问、连续轨迹及有限轨迹线性时序逻辑约束，并给出可认证的近似最优性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统旅行商及其图搜索变体通常假设有限离散节点和固定访问结构，难以直接表达“访问凸区域而非单点”、可选过渡节点、重复经过节点以及连续运动规划之间的耦合。该工作旨在为此类组合—连续联合规划问题构建具有终止保证和全局次优性证书的求解方法。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为GCS上的Steiner-TSP，并在以根节点为起点的路径前缀空间中执行分支定界。对已确定的前缀，使用可加的下界图代价；对尚未访问的目标及返回根节点的剩余代价，采用通过割分离增强的连通流松弛进行估计。搜索可采用最佳优先或深度优先策略。在边代价一致为正的条件下，最佳优先搜索无需初始可行解即可在可行实例上有限终止；深度优先搜索在已有有限上界解时有限终止。算法还以全局下界和用户指定的ε≥1为依据，认证当前解代价至多为全局最优值的ε倍。

Result: 在移动操作臂巡检实验中，框架联合优化传感模式、目标访问顺序和连续轨迹，并支持以LTL_f表示的动作先后约束。两种搜索策略均在30秒内为全部基准实例找到可行解；其平均认证最优性缺口分别为28.1%和29.7%。相比之下，两个近期基线方法仅能在约一半实例上成功求解。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个面向GCS上Steiner-TSP的统一、可证明终止且可提供全局次优性证书的搜索框架。实验表明，该方法在包含逻辑约束和连续规划因素的联合任务中具有更高的可解性与稳定性；但报告的最优性缺口仍约为三成，说明下界松弛质量和搜索效率仍存在进一步改进空间。

Abstract: We formalize the Steiner Traveling Salesman Problem (Steiner-TSP) on Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS), which seeks a minimum-cost closed trajectory through required convex sets while allowing optional transit vertices and revisits. To explore the resulting infinite solution space, we propose a unified branch-and-bound search over rooted walk prefixes. Additive lower-bound-graph costs bound committed prefixes, while a cut-separated connected-flow relaxation lower-bounds the residual cost of visiting every remaining target and returning to the root. Under a uniform positive-cost assumption, best-first traversal terminates after finitely many expansions on every feasible instance without an initial incumbent, whereas depth-first traversal does so once a finite incumbent is available. For a user-specified factor $ε\geq1$, a global lower bound certifies that either strategy's incumbent cost is at most $ε$ times the global optimum. We further demonstrate joint sensing-mode, visitation-order, and continuous-trajectory selection for a mobile-manipulator inspection task, including action precedences expressed in linear temporal logic over finite traces (LTL$_f$). Both traversal strategies find feasible solutions on all benchmark instances within 30s with mean certified optimality gaps of 28.1% and 29.7%, respectively, whereas two recent baselines succeed on only about half of the instances

</details>


### [91] [Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks: Encoding Anatomic Priors in Loss and Architecture, with an SE(3) Formulation of Guidewire-Induced Aortoiliac Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21332)
*David P. Stonko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出解剖学先验神经网络（AINN）框架，将软解剖先验作为损失函数惩罚项，并将可强制实施的硬先验编码进模型架构，以避免解剖上不可能的预测。其临床示例结合连续体力学与二维血管造影监督，预测导丝进入后主动脉—髂动脉树的变形；但当前尚未训练神经网络，也未完成真实CT数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型可能产生数值上合理但解剖学上不可能的结果，且在小样本条件下泛化能力有限。主动脉腔内操作及自主血管导航需要从有限二维影像推断三维血管—导丝耦合变形，因此需要将解剖约束、力学规律和稀疏观测结合起来。

Method: AINN通过两类先验约束预测：软先验以损失惩罚项表达，硬先验则通过架构和状态表示实现，使违反先验的结果在结构上无法生成。具体模型将血管中心线和导丝路径从R^3提升为SE(3)上的标架曲线；采用Cosserat杆描述导丝，并将其与具有曲率调制且解剖锚定的血管模型耦合；通过单侧管腔接触不等式描述接触，耦合弹性能量的受约束最小化给出预测，接触力作为拉格朗日乘子。训练监督采用基于C臂成像几何投影的Wasserstein-2最优传输损失，使二维血管造影能够监督三维预测。

Result: 运动学、损失函数和投影过程已通过已知真值验证；力学求解器仅依据自身最优性条件进行验证，预测位移尚未达到网格收敛。当前工作没有训练神经网络，也没有在真实CT数据上评估预测精度、泛化能力或数据效率。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一个将解剖先验、几何投影和耦合弹性力学统一起来的AINN概念框架，理论上有助于减少解剖学不合理预测并支持小样本三维推断。但现阶段主要是数值建模和方法论验证，尚不能证明其相较现有方法的性能优势；后续需迁移至真实CT扫描，训练网络，并评估预测准确性、网格收敛性及所需训练数据量。

Abstract: Deep-learning models of anatomy can be numerically plausible yet anatomically impossible, and they generalize poorly when data are scarce. We introduce Anatomy-Informed Neural Networks (AINN), in which soft anatomic priors enter as penalty terms in the loss (e.g., a branching penalty that treats a renal transplant artery off the iliac instead of the aorta as unexpected rather than impossible), in direct analogy to a physics-informed neural network, and hard anatomic priors (e.g., continuity of the vessel) are built into the architecture and state representation, making such invalid predictions impossible by construction wherever the prior admits architectural enforcement.
  We develop it on a clinical test case with limited data: how the aortoiliac tree deforms when a stiff wire is introduced endoluminally. This is important to contemporary aortic surgery and will matter to autonomous endovascular navigation. We lift the vessel centerline and the wire path from R^3 to curves of frames in the Lie group SE(3), and couple a Cosserat-rod wire to a tortuosity-modulated, anatomically anchored vessel through a unilateral lumen-contact inequality. The prediction is a constrained minimizer of the coupled elastic energy, with contact forces as its Lagrange multipliers. Supervision is a Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport loss between the predicted projection through the C-arm geometry and the observed angiogram, so a 2D angiogram can train a 3D prediction. The kinematics, loss and projection are verified against known ground truth; the mechanics solver only against its own optimality conditions, and predicted displacement is not yet mesh-converged. Here, no network is trained. Future work will transfer this in silico model to real CT scans and test whether it improves predictive accuracy and reduces the training data required.

</details>


### [92] [VIALS: A Benchmark for Visual Interpretation of Artifacts in the Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21357)
*Elaine Lau,Thanuka Udumulla,Lee Izhaki-Tavor,Francisco Guzmán,Nicholas Magazine,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出VIALS视觉问答基准，用于评估AI对生命科学专业工作流中常见视觉产物的解读能力。基准含161项任务，结果表明前沿视觉语言模型虽能流畅描述自然图像，却难以准确完成此类科学图像解释；具备相关领域知识的科学家则能较容易完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命科学研发高度依赖凝胶印迹、显微图像、质粒图谱、流式细胞术散点图和分子结构等视觉信息来支持实验判断、沟通与决策。现有多模态模型的评估往往侧重论文或教材中的规范化图表，不能充分反映产业实验流程里真实、未润色的视觉解读需求。

Method: 作者构建VIALS基准，收集并设计覆盖生物技术产业实验工作流多类视觉产物的161个视觉问答任务。通过比较前沿视觉语言模型与具有相应专业背景的科学家在这些任务上的表现，考察模型的领域知识掌握程度及面向专业图像的视觉推理能力。

Result: 前沿视觉语言模型在科学视觉产物的准确解释方面表现不足，暴露出领域知识缺失和领域特异性视觉推理能力有限的问题。相较之下，具备相关专业知识的科学家能够较顺利地完成这些解释任务，显示该基准所测能力具有明确的专业实践基础。

Conclusion: 若AI不能可靠解读生命科学工作流中的核心视觉产物，其在专业生命科学场景中的实际价值将受到限制。VIALS为识别和推动改进多模态模型的科学图像理解、专业知识整合与实验推理能力提供了面向真实工作流的评测工具。

Abstract: In professional life sciences workflows, scientists routinely interpret visual artifacts (gel blots, microscopy images, plasmid maps, flow cytometry plots, molecular structures, ...) to inform research decisions. We introduce VIALS, a visual question-answering benchmark with 161 such interpretation tasks, spanning the types of artifacts examined throughout experimental workflows in the biotech industry (rather than polished figures from publications and textbooks). While frontier vision-language models can now fluently describe natural images, we find that they are unable to accurately interpret these scientific images, reflecting limitations in domain knowledge and domain-specific visual reasoning capabilities. In contrast, scientists with relevant domain expertise find these visual interpretation tasks straightforward. AI that cannot similarly interpret such images will have limited utility in professional life sciences workflows, where such artifacts are central to how scientists reason, communicate, and make decisions.

</details>

### [93] [ProofJudge: Tool-Grounded LLM Evaluation of Formal Proof Quality in Mathlib](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20432)
*Shane Caldwell*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出ProofJudge：一个具备工具访问能力的智能体式大语言模型评审系统，用于评估Lean 4形式化证明在“通过内核检查”之外的工程与风格质量。实验显示，其评分与Mathlib人工审稿偏好具有较高一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lean 4证明的正确性由类型检查器保证，但正确证明之间仍可能在复用现有库、自动化策略、代码结构、定理陈述及社区规范遵循方面存在显著质量差异。缺少可扩展且贴近人工审稿标准的自动评估机制，限制了证明生成系统的优化与比较。

Method: 作者构建ProofJudge，并从218个不同Mathlib拉取请求中抽取声明形成评测集。系统沿五个维度评分：库资源利用、自动化适配性、结构清晰度、陈述质量和Mathlib规范一致性。评审智能体可访问对应提交版本的库状态，以查询实际可用定义、引理和约定。对齐指标定义为：若模型将最终被Mathlib接受的版本排在最初被要求修改的版本之前，则视为与人工偏好一致。

Result: 六个被评估的评审模型均显著优于随机猜测，对人工审稿偏好的恢复准确率介于63.5%至80.8%。其中两个开放权重模型的表现约为70%，而成本仅约为最佳模型的十分之一，表明较低成本的本地或开放模型也具备实用价值。作者同时开源了评审框架、评测数据集和完整评测轨迹。

Conclusion: ProofJudge表明，结合代码库查询能力的LLM评审器可以有效近似形式化证明社区的人类质量判断。该工作将证明评估从单一正确性扩展至可维护性、惯例和库复用等维度，并提供了可复现基础设施；但其验证范围主要基于Mathlib修改前后的成对偏好，跨项目泛化能力、五个维度评分的独立可靠性以及对证明生成实际改进效果仍需进一步检验。

Abstract: Formal proofs in Lean 4 that pass the kernel's type checker can nonetheless vary widely in quality. We introduce ProofJudge, an agentic LLM-as-judge system that scores formal proof quality along five dimensions beyond correctness: library leverage, automation fit, structural clarity, statement quality, and Mathlib conventions. We evaluate ProofJudge on a novel dataset of 218 declarations drawn from distinct Mathlib PRs. The judge agent is grounded by tool access to the commit the PR is applied to, enabling it to query the library state when scoring. A judge is considered aligned with human preferences when it rates the version of the PR Mathlib accepted above the initial version that was sent back for revision. All six judge models evaluated recover the reviewers' preference well above chance, from 80.8% to 63.5%, and two open-weight judges reach roughly 70% at a tenth of the best judge's cost. We release the judge harness, evaluation dataset, and evaluation traces as open-source artifacts to support further research.

</details>


### [94] [Runtime Verification under Split Past and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20783)
*Dogan Ulus*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出面向自主系统运行时保障的形式化框架：将已观测执行历史与多个预测未来轨迹联合分析，并以新逻辑SplitLTL表达历史驱动的当前保障要求及其对未来轨迹的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运行时验证主要判断截至当前时刻的执行是否满足性质，难以直接处理系统决策所需的未来行为预测。自主系统需要根据已发生的上下文动态确定当前约束，并筛选满足这些约束的可能后续轨迹。

Method: 作者定义SplitLTL的语法与语义，将一个规范划分为过去部分和未来部分：过去部分在观测历史上求值，用于推导当前时刻适用的保障要求；未来部分在各条预测延续上求值，检验其是否符合该要求。随后构建在线监控架构，同时接收实时执行历史和多个预测延续，并输出可接受的延续集合。

Result: 该框架能够依据观测历史对预测轨迹进行条件化过滤，识别与当前上下文一致且满足保障要求的可采纳未来行为，从而为后续决策提供形式化依据。摘要未报告实验数据、性能指标或与现有方法的定量比较。

Conclusion: SplitLTL为“过去决定约束、未来接受验证”的运行时保障范式提供了统一的时序逻辑表达和在线评估机制，扩展了传统运行时验证仅关注已发生执行的能力；其实际效果仍需通过实现、复杂度分析及真实场景评测进一步验证。

Abstract: Runtime assurance for autonomous systems increasingly requires reasoning not only about observed executions but also about anticipated future behaviors. Traditional runtime verification, however, primarily evaluates the execution observed so far and does not directly account for predicted continuations. We propose a formal framework for runtime assurance that combines monitoring of the observed execution with analysis of multiple predicted continuations. To support this integration, we introduce Split Linear Temporal Logic (SplitLTL), a linear-time temporal logic that assigns complementary roles to past and future temporal specifications within a single specification. The past component uses the observed history to determine the assurance requirements applicable at the current point of execution, while the future component evaluates predicted continuations against those requirements. The framework therefore filters predicted continuations according to the requirements induced by the observed history, identifying admissible continuations that can support subsequent decision making. We formally define the syntax and semantics of SplitLTL and present an online monitoring architecture for evaluating observed executions together with predicted continuations.

</details>


### [95] [Extensions of Courcelle's Theorem without Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21081)
*Yuval Filmus,Johann A. Makowsky*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文将Courcelle定理中的单子二阶逻辑可定义性，替换为基于广义连接矩阵的纯组合条件，从而得到适用于有界树宽图的更一般线性时间算法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Courcelle定理依赖逻辑可定义性，难以直接揭示其算法成立的本质。受Lovász利用连接矩阵研究图性质的工作启发，本文旨在厘清逻辑在此类定理中的作用，并提取其背后的组合结构。

Method: 构造并研究连接矩阵的推广形式，以刻画图性质在图拼接操作下的有限组合行为；在给定大小为t(G)的树分解、且图树宽至多为k的条件下，用该组合条件取代单子二阶逻辑可定义性，并建立相应的动态规划算法。

Result: 论文证明了一个广义Courcelle定理：满足广义连接矩阵所表达的组合条件的图性质，可以在线性于树分解规模t(G)的时间内进行判定。该结果将逻辑假设转化为纯组合假设，并概括了经典定理的适用范围。

Conclusion: Courcelle型线性时间结果的核心并不必然是单子二阶逻辑，而是图性质在有限树分解结构和拼接操作下所具有的有限组合表示性。广义连接矩阵为分析和推广此类算法定理提供了统一工具。

Abstract: Courcelle's Theorem states that on graphs $G$ of tree-width at most $k$ with a given tree-decomposition of size $t(G)$, graph properties $\mathcal{P}$ definable in Monadic Second Order Logic can be checked in linear time in the size of $t(G)$. Inspired by L. Lovász' work using connection matrices instead of logic, we give a generalized version of Courcelle's theorem which replaces the definability hypothesis by a purely combinatorial hypothesis using a generalization of connection matrices. This paper clarifies the role of logic in such theorems and displays their purely combinatorial assumption.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [AEGIS: Preventing Cross-Domain Resource Abuse in MCP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20481)
*Shriti Priya,Teryl Taylor,Frederico Araujo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出AEGIS，一种面向模型上下文协议（MCP）的资源滥用策略执行组件。其核心目标是将异构、多模态工具调用归一化为统一的策略表示，并结合大语言模型、Open Policy Agent和ContextForge AI Gateway实施细粒度资源约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP通过工具调用连接大语言模型与外部系统，但文本、图像、视频和位置等模态的请求参数差异显著。例如，过大的搜索范围或过长的视频请求可能造成后端资源过载。现有方法难以同时实现跨工具、跨模态的通用性与精确的资源限制，因此需要一种可由管理者配置且易于扩展的统一治理机制。

Method: AEGIS利用大语言模型的推理能力解析工具调用，识别请求类别、参数语义及潜在资源消耗，并将不同MCP工具的异构调用模式转换为面向策略的统一表示。在执行层面，系统与Open Policy Agent集成，根据管理员定义的规则评估调用是否满足资源约束；同时通过ContextForge AI Gateway在工具访问路径中执行检测、拦截或限制。

Result: 摘要声称，AEGIS能够在不牺牲MCP智能体生态灵活性的前提下，对异构工具和多模态调用中的资源滥用行为进行检测与缓解。其主要贡献是提供跨模态、跨领域的调用归一化与细粒度策略执行能力。摘要未给出具体实验设置、基准任务、检测精度、性能开销或资源节省等量化结果。

Conclusion: AEGIS将大语言模型驱动的调用语义理解与基于策略的访问控制结合，为MCP工具生态中的资源治理提供了通用框架。该工作的价值取决于其归一化表示的可靠性、策略规则对新工具的泛化能力，以及大语言模型分析环节的延迟、成本和稳定性；这些关键问题仍需结合完整论文中的实验进一步验证。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is an open source JSON-RPC protocol that standardizes how large language models (LLMs) interact with external systems through programmatic functions known as tools. Attackers or malicious agents can exploit certain modalities of these MCP tools to degrade the overall quality of service of agent-based applications. For example, an agent may request an excessively large search radius or very long videos, overloading backend systems and potentially causing slowdowns or denial-of-service. Each modality including text, images, video, and location introduces distinct vectors for resource abuse, complicating the development of consistent mitigation strategies. Moreover, multimodal and crossdomain tools expose diverse request schemas and parameters, making it difficult to define policies that are both generalizable and precise enough to enforce meaningful resource constraints. In this paper, we present AEGIS, a policy enforcement component that enables administrators to define fine-grained safeguards against resource abuse across heterogeneous MCP tools and modalities. AEGIS leverages the reasoning capabilities of large language models to analyze, categorize, and normalize diverse tool invocations into a unified, policy-friendly representation accessible to security practitioners. Integrated with the Open Policy Agent and the ContextForge AI Gateway, AEGIS detects and mitigates abusive behaviors while preserving the flexibility of MCP-based agent ecosystems.

</details>


### [97] [aiXamine: Unified Black-Box Evaluation of Cross-Dimensional Trade-offs in LLM Safety, Security, and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20554)
*Fatih Deniz,Yazan Boshmaf,Dorde Popovic,Issa Khalil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出aiXamine统一黑盒评测平台，跨安全性、安保性与隐私性评估大语言模型的可信度，并揭示单一维度优化可能损害其他维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测通常将安全性、安保性和隐私性分开衡量，无法发现过度拒答、隐私退化及鲁棒性崩溃等跨维度权衡与失效模式，因此需要统一、可复现的综合评测框架。

Method: aiXamine通过自动化红队流程，协调9类服务中的46项测试，生成从提示级诊断到跨服务权衡分析的分层风险画像。研究对120多个大语言模型开展超过5000次测试，并在相同条件下比较闭源和开放权重模型。

Result: 研究发现三类现象：更强的安全对齐会带来可量化的安全性代价，即过度拒答增加并损害可用性；隐私性与其他可信度维度近似正交，无法由标准对齐水平充分预测；无策略蒸馏且缺少在线策略校正会导致熵塌缩，使同一基础架构上的鲁棒性由56.9骤降至2.6。此外，模型规模带来的收益存在边际递减，安全行为依赖任务类别。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可信度具有本质上的多维性，单一轴向的对齐优化既不能保证整体可信度，还可能主动削弱其他维度。模型开发与评测应采用联合指标、跨维度风险分析及针对蒸馏过程的在线策略校正。

Abstract: The critical failure modes in deployed large language models (LLMs) are cross-dimensional: a model can score 99.3 in safety alignment while refusing one in three benign queries, or improve across every capability metric while losing 21 points in privacy. Existing evaluation frameworks that assess safety, security, and privacy independently cannot detect these patterns. We introduce aiXamine, a unified black-box platform that evaluates LLM trustworthiness across safety, security, and privacy as interdependent properties. aiXamine orchestrates 46 tests across nine services through an automated red-teaming pipeline, producing hierarchical risk profiles, from prompt-level diagnostics to cross-service trade-off analytics, that enable reproducible comparison of proprietary and open-weight systems under identical conditions. Applying aiXamine to over 120 LLMs through more than 5,000 test runs, we conduct the largest joint safety, security, and privacy study to date and uncover three cross-dimensional phenomena invisible to single-axis evaluation. First, safety enforcement incurs a quantifiable safety tax: stronger alignment systematically increases over-refusal, forcing providers to choose between protection and utility. Second, privacy is near-orthogonal to other trustworthiness dimensions and not captured by standard alignment. Third, we identify and formally characterize distillation-induced robustness collapse: off-policy distillation without on-policy correction causes entropy collapse, catastrophically destroying robustness (56.9$\to$2.6) on the same base architecture. These findings, compounded by diminishing returns from scale and category-dependent safety behaviors, demonstrate that trustworthiness is inherently multi-dimensional: progress along one axis does not guarantee, and can actively undermine, progress along others, yet current alignment methods treat it as a single objective.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond End-to-End Success: Diagnosing Failures in Long-Horizon Security LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20563)
*Wei Shao,Chongzhou Fang,Zuxiong Tan,Zequan Liang,Setareh Rafatirad,Avesta Sasan,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向长时程安全类LLM智能体的诊断评测方法：通过任务检查点、能力暴露前后失败分离和受控干预，定位智能体失败发生的位置及原因。实验表明，不同模型代际的主要失败瓶颈可能显著变化，单一任务成功率不足以解释能力缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务包含相互依赖的多轮交互，后续行动依赖早期发现的信息、状态或访问条件。仅观察最终成功与否会混淆两类失败：智能体可能尚未到达可施展目标能力的阶段即已失败，因而无法判断失败究竟源于能力不足还是上游流程受阻。

Method: 方法包括三部分：（1）在任务流程中设置检查点，记录关键信息发现、状态观察和策略转换等中间事件；（2）按能力是否已暴露，将失败划分为暴露前失败与暴露后失败；（3）针对怀疑的上游瓶颈施加预先设计的受控干预，并与匹配对照比较。作者在四类任务中评估该框架：延迟复用已发现信息、复用已观察状态、失败策略后的恢复，以及不确定结果后的决策。

Result: 在“已观察状态复用”任务中，检查点分析发现，Gemini 2.5 Flash的大量失败发生在模型观察到后续需要复用的状态之前。在预先规定的92个随机种子实验中，加入针对协议歧义的指导后，状态观察率从匹配的无指导对照条件下的65.5%提高至95.4%。但同一实验设计用于Gemini 3.7 Flash时得到相反效果；同时，状态观察已不再稳定预测最终任务完成率。

Conclusion: 长时程智能体评测应从终局成功率导向转为过程诊断导向。检查点和干预实验能够区分能力暴露前后的失败，并验证上游瓶颈假设；而模型更新可能改变失败机制，因此不能将某一模型上的诊断结论直接外推到其他模型代际。

Abstract: Long-horizon security LLM agents must carry information and decisions across many dependent interactions, where later actions often depend on services, state, or access discovered much earlier. This makes final task success difficult to interpret: an agent may fail before it ever reaches the point where the capability of interest can be exercised. We present a diagnostic methodology that instruments security tasks with checkpoints, separates failures before and after capability exposure, and uses controlled interventions to test suspected upstream bottlenecks. We evaluate the methodology across four task families involving delayed reuse of discovered information, reuse of observed state, recovery from failed strategies, and decision making after uncertain outcomes. On observed state reuse, checkpoint analysis shows that many Gemini 2.5 Flash failures occur before the model observes the state it is later expected to reuse. In a pre-specified 92-seed study, targeted protocol-disambiguation guidance increases state observation from 65.5\% under a matched non-guidance control message to 95.4\%. Repeating the same design with Gemini 3.7 Flash produces the opposite effect, while state observation no longer reliably predicts task completion. These results show that the dominant source of failure can shift across model generations, motivating evaluation that diagnoses where and why long-horizon security agents fail rather than relying only on aggregate task success.

</details>


### [99] [Temporal Risk on Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20575)
*Shiqi Liu,Kun Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向卫星安全的时序风险评估框架，将时间作为核心分析维度，以替代传统框架中静态、单一的风险判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星轨道、供电余量与空间环境会随任务进程变化，导致同一安全利用行为在不同任务阶段具有显著不同的可行性与影响。然而，现有风险框架通常未充分描述这种动态性。

Method: 框架扩展既有对抗行为分类体系，引入五维时序能力模型；将任务划分为具有操作意义的时间窗口及环境分段，分别估计各窗口中的利用难度、发生可能性与影响，并生成按时间索引的可能性—影响矩阵序列。

Result: 该方法不再输出单一风险分数，而是揭示卫星系统在不同任务时段和环境条件下的风险暴露变化，可识别高风险窗口及风险随时间迁移的特征。

Conclusion: 时序化风险分析能够支持运营方避开高风险时段安排敏感操作，并依据动态威胁态势调配防御资源，从而提升卫星任务安全管理的针对性与有效性。

Abstract: Satellite vulnerabilities change over time as orbits shift, power margins tighten, and the space environment deteriorates. However, most cybersecurity risk frameworks still treat threats as static. In practice, the same exploit can be far more damaging during a critical maneuver than during routine operations. We propose a temporal risk assessment framework that makes time an explicit axis in satellite security analysis. It extends existing adversary behavior taxonomies with a five-dimensional temporal capability model and estimates exploitation difficulty across distinct temporal windows of a mission. Rather than producing a single risk score, the framework outputs a series of time-indexed likelihood-impact matrices. It discretizes missions into operationally meaningful time windows and environmental bands to show when systems are most exposed. This view helps operators avoid scheduling sensitive operations in high-risk periods and align defensive resources with a threat landscape that shifts over time.

</details>


### [100] [Keyed Provenance Watermarking with Complementary Lattice-Based Secure Aggregation for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20580)
*Xinyun Liu,Zhi Lu,Yu Chen,Ronghua Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出双层可信联邦学习框架：数据层以物理锚点元数据生成带密钥的溯源水印，计算层以基于格的零知识安全聚合验证梯度合规性，旨在同时降低数据泄露、未授权复用与恶意梯度篡改风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习防护通常分别处理数据溯源、水印鲁棒性或梯度安全聚合，缺乏覆盖数据来源可信性与训练过程可验证性的端到端协同机制。论文试图弥补这一割裂，构建面向复合威胁的统一验证流程。

Method: 数据层中，利用时间、位置和服务器标识等可信基础设施信息构造物理锚点元数据（PAM），再经客户端秘密密钥驱动的HMAC-SHA-256生成水印载荷；随后通过含特征融合模块与Mamba引导线性注意力的FMGAN，将载荷鲁棒嵌入图像。计算层中，采用基于RLWE困难问题的格零知识安全聚合协议，在不暴露本地梯度的前提下验证密钥正确性、L2范数上界及余弦相似度约束。

Result: 摘要宣称，大量实验表明两层机制在复合场景下具有互补保护效果，并称其为首个同时评估数据层水印溯源与计算层可验证安全聚合的混合式端到端可信联邦学习验证工作流。摘要未提供具体数据集、攻击配置、性能指标、通信开销、验证成本或与基线方法的定量比较，因此实际增益尚无法独立判断。

Conclusion: 该工作的重要性在于将带密钥、符合Kerckhoffs原则的数据溯源水印，与后量子安全的零知识梯度约束验证相结合。其核心贡献具有系统整合价值，但论文说服力将取决于：PAM可信来源与隐私处理、FMGAN在压缩和再训练等扰动下的水印鲁棒性、零知识协议的正确性与开销，以及双层方案相对于单层基线的可量化收益。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is vulnerable to multi-level attacks. However, existing methods address them separately, leaving FL exposed to data leakage, unauthorized reuse, and malicious gradient manipulation. In this work, we propose an FL framework that couples keyed context-provenance watermarking with verifiable lattice-based secure aggregation of Real-World Anchored Watermarking and Lattice-Based Zero-Knowledge Secure Aggregation. At the data layer, we propose a Kerckhoffs-compliant scheme that utilizes Physical Anchor Metadata (PAM) to ensure data provenance. PAM is defined as a context-provenance token derived from trusted infrastructure data (time, location, and server ID) and then subjected to a keyed HMAC-SHA-256 transformation to produce a watermark payload that cannot be generated without the client's secret key. We further design FMGAN, a GAN-based robust image watermarking framework that embeds this transformed payload using a feature fusion module and a Mamba-guided linear attention mechanism. At the computation layer, we adopt a lattice-based zero-knowledge secure aggregation (LZKSA) protocol that verifies key correctness, L2 norm bounds, and cosine similarity constraints over committed gradients without revealing private updates. The RLWE-based design guarantees post-quantum security. Extensive experiments validate the complementary protection of the two layers under composite attack scenarios. To our knowledge, no prior verification workflow has jointly evaluated both layers in a hybrid, end-to-end trustworthy FL framework.

</details>


### [101] [MATEE: Efficiently Bridging the Semantic Gap in TrustZone via Arm Pointer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20583)
*Shiqi Liu,Xiang Li,Jie Wang,Yongpeng Gao,Jiajin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文聚焦Arm TrustZone中安全世界与普通世界之间的语义鸿沟：安全世界难以可靠识别普通世界请求的真实客户端身份，导致跨客户端敏感数据访问风险。作者提出MATEE，利用Arm指针认证将请求与客户端身份绑定，并在访问时验证，以较低开销缓解此类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TrustZone的隔离主要区分安全世界与普通世界，但普通世界内部不同客户端应用之间的身份语义无法被安全世界充分感知。若可信应用仅依据可伪造或精心构造的请求参数授权，恶意客户端可能冒充或混淆身份，从而访问其他客户端关联的敏感数据。

Method: 作者首先系统化归类语义鸿沟漏洞（SGVs），再设计MATEE作为TEE侧防护机制。MATEE借助Arm Pointer Authentication（PA）对每个请求施加与发起客户端身份关联的认证绑定；可信应用处理涉及敏感数据的访问时验证该绑定，从而识别请求所属客户端。该方案旨在实现不同客户端敏感数据的隔离，并避免修改既有客户端应用和可信应用。

Result: 摘要声称MATEE能够阻止由请求伪造引发的SGVs，并在无需改动现有客户端应用与可信应用的前提下隔离不同客户端的敏感数据。实验报告的运行时开销为2.19%，表明其性能代价较低。

Conclusion: 该工作将TrustZone跨世界语义缺失具体化为客户端身份认证问题，并以硬件指针认证机制建立请求来源的可验证绑定。其主要贡献在于漏洞分类、透明化防护设计及低开销评估；但摘要未说明其对硬件PA覆盖范围、密钥与生命周期管理、请求链路完整性及复杂共享场景的适用边界，仍需结合全文进一步判断。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) employ hardware-based isolation mechanisms to safeguard the confidentiality and integrity of sensitive code and data. One such prevalent implementation is Arm TrustZone, which partitions the system into the secure and normal (non-secure) worlds. However, this partitioning results in the secure world having very limited visibility into the operating information of the normal world, creating a semantic gap between these two worlds. Specifically, the secure world lacks an effective user identity authentication when receiving data requests from the normal world. Consequently, malicious Client Applications (CAs) in the normal world can deceive Trusted Applications (TAs) in the secure world by utilizing elaborate request parameters, compromising the sensitive data stored by other CAs. We systematically classify these Semantic Gap Vulnerabilities (SGVs) and propose a mate system for the TEE called MATEE to defend against SGVs. MATEE utilizes Arm Pointer Authentication (PA) to bind each request to the corresponding CA's identity and then verifies the identity when the CA accesses sensitive data, thereby preventing malicious request forgery. In particular, MATEE isolates sensitive data of different CAs without modifying existing CAs and TAs. Our evaluation demonstrates that MATEE successfully defends against SGVs with a minimal runtime overhead (2.19%).

</details>


### [102] [More Granular, Less Trust: Enforcing Intra-Process Isolation with Arm CCA in an Untrusted Management Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20584)
*Shiqi Liu,Zhouqi Jiang,Jie Wang,Wei Zhou,Kun Sun,Zhaohui Chen,Yulai Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出CCAegis：一种基于Arm CCA的进程内细粒度隔离系统。它通过静态分析识别敏感数据及其处理函数，并在函数调用/返回处切换Granule Protection Table（GPT）权限，以抵御操作系统和进程内非授权代码对敏感数据的访问；在真实密码学工作负载中，性能开销为原始版本的1.01倍至1.43倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密虚拟机可降低对云平台的信任，但操作系统仍拥有较高权限，且现有进程级隔离无法覆盖进程内部的非授权访问。已有进程内隔离方案往往依赖操作系统创建、管理或执行隔离域，导致可信计算基过大。因此，需要一种更细粒度、且不依赖操作系统可信性的隔离机制。

Method: CCAegis扩展Arm CCA的保护机制。系统首先使用静态分析追踪敏感数据流，并定位读写或处理敏感数据的函数；随后在相关函数的调用点和返回点插入权限切换指令，通过修改GPT中的访问权限，使敏感数据仅能被授权函数访问。系统将GPT配置和隔离域切换交由Secure Monitor负责，操作系统不参与可信权限管理，从而将信任边界收缩至Secure Monitor。

Result: 作者在官方模拟器和真实开发板上实现并评估CCAegis。结果表明，该系统能够隔离敏感数据及相关操作，防止操作系统与进程内非授权代码访问受保护对象。在真实密码学工作负载中，相对未保护版本的运行时间开销介于1.01倍至1.43倍之间。

Conclusion: CCAegis通过函数粒度的GPT权限动态切换，实现了面向机密虚拟机的进程内隔离，并减少了对操作系统的信任。其主要价值在于以较小可信计算基保护敏感数据路径；但摘要未说明静态分析对间接调用、动态加载代码、复杂别名关系及异常控制流的覆盖程度，这些因素可能影响方案的完整性与适用范围。

Abstract: With the increasing adoption of confidential computing, security-sensitive applications are often deployed in confidential virtual machines (CVMs), which reduce reliance on third-party cloud providers. However, privilege attacks originating from the OS remain a significant threat in these environments. Existing finer-grained isolation schemes, such as SHELTER, provide process-level protection but are still vulnerable to intraprocess attacks and potential collusion between the OS and intra-process adversaries. Many current intra-process isolation techniques continue to depend on the OS to manage and enforce isolation domains, leading to a large Trusted Computing Base (TCB). This gap highlights the need for more granular, less trust-dependent confidential computing solutions. In this paper, we present CCAegis, a system that extends the Arm Confidential Compute Architecture (CCA) to enforce intra-process isolation of sensitive data and operations, safeguarding them from both intraprocess adversaries and the OS. We employ static analysis to track the flow of sensitive data and identify functions that handle such data. Permission-switching instructions are inserted at the function call and return points, adjusting permissions via the Granule Protection Table (GPT) to ensure that only designated functions can access the isolated data. Notably, CCAegis places trust solely in the Secure Monitor, which configures the GPTs and manages domain switching, thereby minimizing the TCB. We implemented CCAegis on both an official emulator and a real development board to assess its performance. Our experimental results show that CCAegis effectively isolates sensitive data and operations, with performance overheads ranging from 1.01x to 1.43x compared to the original version across real-world cryptographic workloads.

</details>


### [103] [ARQ: Agentic CodeQL Query Refinement for C/C++ Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20637)
*Chunyi Wang,Yunfei Ke,Junfeng Yang,Yun-Yun Tsai,Penghui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ARQ是一种基于执行证据和大语言模型的自动化框架，用于改进C/C++ CodeQL漏洞检测查询，在保持高精度的同时显著提升真实漏洞检出率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于查询的静态分析器容易产生误报和漏报，而传统查询改进方法通常依赖标注数据、提交历史或特定漏洞模板，限制了自动化和通用性。

Method: ARQ首先合成C/C++程序并执行，再将程序的实际安全状态与CodeQL查询结果进行对比：真实存在漏洞但查询未报告时识别为漏报缺陷，程序安全但查询触发时识别为误报缺陷。随后，基于大语言模型的迭代流程利用这些执行不一致证据自动修复查询，无需标注数据、提交历史或漏洞专用模板。研究使用GPT-5.4、Claude-Sonnet-4.6和Gemini-3.5-flash改进12个官方CodeQL查询，并在Juliet v1.3和FormAI v2数据集上进行评估。

Result: ARQ改进后的查询在保持至少98.0%精确率的同时，真实阳性检出量最高提升119.8%。该方法还修复了官方CodeQL查询仓库中3个长期未解决的问题，这些问题最长已存在27个月；此外，在libpng和zlib中发现了两个此前未公开的缺陷。

Conclusion: 执行驱动的程序合成证据能够有效揭示静态分析查询的误报与漏报弱点，并指导大语言模型进行自动修复。ARQ在无需人工标注和领域特定模板的条件下显著提升了CodeQL查询的实用性与漏洞检测能力。

Abstract: Static analyzers have been widely adopted for vulnerability detection in C/C++ programs. Query-based static analyzers (e.g., CodeQL) encode vulnerable code patterns in detection queries and match them against source code. However, existing queries still suffer from false positives (FPs, incorrectly flagging benign code as vulnerable) and false negatives (FNs, missing real vulnerabilities). We present ARQ, an agentic framework that automatically refines C/C++ CodeQL queries using execution-grounded evidence from synthesized C/C++ programs. Our key insight is that a synthesized program exposes a query's weakness whenever its execution disagrees with the query's verdict. If the program is genuinely vulnerable but the query stays silent, the query has an FN weakness; if the program is safe but the query fires anyway, it has an FP weakness. ARQ then runs an LLM-based refinement loop that repairs the query using these disagreements as ground truth. Unlike previous query refining methods, ARQ requires no labeled datasets, no commit history, and no vulnerability-specific templates. We demonstrate the effectiveness of ARQ by refining 12 official CodeQL queries using three commercial LLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude-Sonnet-4.6, and Gemini-3.5-flash). We compare both ARQ-refined and original CodeQL queries on the Juliet v1.3 and FormAI v2 datasets and show that ARQ-refined queries detect substantially more true positives, by up to 119.8\%, with a Precision of at least 98.0\% throughout. ARQ successfully fixed three unresolved GitHub issues raised in the official CodeQL query repository that had remained open for as long as \textit{27 months}.
